# Audit report

Made by scripts/audit-report.cjs from audit/results. Rules and file format: docs/AUDIT.md.

- Films checked: 900 of 900
- Lines checked and right: 12490
- Wrong lines still open: 0 (fixed: 1443)
- Unsure lines still open: 0
- Average vibe: 3.65 out of 5
- Flat menus (vibe under 3.5) waiting for Josh's call: 204
